The two fighters will be participating in the tournament alongside includes [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] and [[Chingiz Allazov]].{{Cite web |date=2024-04-12 |title=ONE Championship to Hold Featherweight Kickboxing Grand Prix in 2024 Featuring Chingiz Allazov & Superbon |url=https://beyondkick.com/news/one-championship-to-hold-featherweight-kickboxing-grand-prix-in-2024-featuring-chingiz-allazov-and-superbon/ |access-date=2024-05-21 |website=Beyond Kickboxing |language=en-US}}\n\n==ONE Friday Fights 47==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 47: Suakim vs. Balyko\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2024|01|12}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]]\n|city=[[Bangkok]], Thailand \n|previousevent=[[ONE Friday Fights 46|ONE Friday Fights 46: Tawanchai vs. Superbon]] \n|followingevent=[[ONE Fight Night 18|ONE Fight Night 18: Gasanov Vs. Oh]] \n}}\n\n'''''ONE Friday Fights 47: Suakim vs. Balyko''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 47''''') was a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on January 12, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\n\nA 140-pound catchweight muay thai bout between former three-division [[Lumpinee Stadium]] world champion [[Suakim PK Saenchaimuaythaigym|Suakim Sor.Jor.Tongprajin]] and Alexey Balyko served as the event headliner.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Alexey Balyko, Kompetch Fairtex, Khunsuk Sor.Dechapan, Numpangna EagleMuayThai''' and '''Apiwat Sor.Somnuk'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! \n| \n|-\n|}\n\n==ONE Friday Fights 58==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 58: Superbon vs. Grigorian 3\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2024|04|5}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]]\n|city=[[Bangkok]], Thailand \n|previousevent=[[ONE Friday Fights 57|ONE Friday Fights 57: Panrit vs. Balyko]] \n|followingevent=[[ONE Fight Night 21|ONE Fight Night 21: Eersel vs. Nicolas]] \n}}\n\n'''''ONE Friday Fights 58: Superbon vs. Grigorian 3''''' (also known as '''''ONE Lumpinee 58''''') is a [[combat sport]]s event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on April 5, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\n\nA trilogy bout for the interim [[ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Championship]] between former champion [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] and former [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Lightweight Championship|Glory Lightweight]] champion [[Marat Grigorian]] headlined the event. The pairing first met at [[2018 in Kunlun Fight#Kunlun Fight 69|Kunlun Fight 69]] on February 4, 2018, where Grigorian won via knockout in the first-round. Their second meeting took place at [[ONE: X]] on March 25, 2022, which ended in a unanimous decision in favor of Superbon.\n\nA [[ONE Strawweight Kickboxing World Championship]] bout between reigning champion [[Jonathan Di Bella]] and reigning [[ONE Strawweight Muay Thai World Championship|ONE Strawweight Muay Thai]] World champion [[Prajanchai P.K.Saenchaimuaythaigym|Prajanchai P.K.Saenchai]] was expected to serve as co-main event. However, as Di Bella was unable to reach the required hydration levels at the weigh-ins, the fight was cancelled and Di Bella was stripped of his title.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $20,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Shadow Singmawynn'''\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Nakrob Fairtex''' and '''Muangthai P.K.Saenchai'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | '''ONE Friday Fights 58 (PPV at Watch ONE)'''\n|-\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Weight Class\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Method\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Round\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Time\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Notes\n|-\n|Featherweight Kickboxing\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] \n|def.\n|{{flagicon|ARM}} [[Marat Grigorian]] \n|Decision (unanimous) \n|align=center| 5 \n|align=center| 3:00 \n|For the interim [[ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Championship]]. \n|-\n|Bantamweight Muay Thai\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Nong-O Gaiyanghadao|Nong-O Hama]]\n|def.\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Kulabdam Sor.Jor.Piek-U-Thai]] \n|Decision (unanimous) \n|align=center| 3 \n|align=center| 3:00 \n| \n|-\n|Catchweight (142 lbs) Muay Thai\n|{{flagicon|JPN}} Yutaro Asahi \n|def.\n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Saeksan Or. event\n|name=ONE Friday Fights 62: Mongkolkaew vs. ET 2\n|image=\n|image_size=320\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{Start date|2024|05|10}}\n|venue=[[Lumpinee Boxing May 10, 2024, at [[Lumpinee Boxing Stadium]] in [[Bangkok]], Thailand.\n\n=== Background ===\nA featherweight muay thai bout between the 2022 RWS welterweight winner [[Shadow Singmawynn|Shadow Singha Mawynn]] and former [[ONE Featherweight Muay Thai World Championship]] challenger [[Jimmy Vienot]] was expected to headline the event. However, Shadow withdrew due to illness and the bout was scrapped.\n\nA 130-pounds catchweight muay thai bout between Mongkolkaew Sor.Sommai and ET Wankhongohm MBK headlined the event.\n\nFurkan Karaba\u011f was expected to face Mavlonbek Kakhkhorov in a featherweight muay thai bout at headline international card, but he pulled out in a last minute due to illness. The headlined of the international card was promoted a 127-pounds catchweight muay thai between Wei Ziqin vs. Riamu Matsumoto instead.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 Sor.Jor.Piek-U-Thai]] and [[Kiamran Nabati]] was expected to headline the event. However, Kulabdam withdrew due to finger injury and Nabati was rescheduled to rematch against Suablack Tor.Pran49 at [[#ONE Friday Fights 68|ONE Friday Fights 68]]. As a results, a bantamweight muay thai bout between Parham Gheirati and Jordan Godtfredsen promoted to main event status.\n\n=== Bonus awards ===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 Segawa excited to finally fight at Lumpinee Stadium \n|url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/article/3277581/one-championship-takeru-segawa-excited-finally-fight-lumpinee-stadium Saenchaimuaythaigym|Muangthai P.K.Saenchai]].{{Cite web |date=2024-10-21 |title=Full Card Revealed for ONE Friday Fights 84 on October 25 \n|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/full-fight-card-revealed-for-one-friday-fights-84-on-october-25/ |access-date=2024-10-24 |website=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home Of Martial Arts |language=en-US}}\n\n===Bonus awards===\nThe following fighters received $10,000 bonuses:\n*'''Performance of the Night: Muangthai P.K.Saenchai, Banluelok Sitwatcharachai, Kochasit Tasaeyasat''' and '''Parham Gheirati'''\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|- \n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | '''ONE Friday Fights 84 (YouTube / Watch ONE)'''\n|-\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Weight Class\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | \n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Method\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Round\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Time\n! colspan=\"1\" style=\"background-color: #E6E8FA; color: #000000; text-align: center;\" | Notes\n|-\n|Catchweight (137 lbs) Muay Thai \n|{{flagicon|THA}} [[Muangthai P.K.
